Result: 在两个消费领域中，仅在解释里披露可持续性信息不会显著提升可持续选项的选择率；对信息进行行为框架化呈现，或加入描述性社会规范，均能显著促进可持续选择，并使用户更容易作出决策。研究还发现感知评价与实际行为存在脱节：普通披露能够改善用户对解释的主观评价，但未能转化为更可持续的选择行为。

Conclusion: 推荐解释的有效性不应仅以用户主观评价衡量，还应考察其对真实选择行为的影响。理论驱动的框架化信息和社会规范提示比单纯信息透明化更适合作为可持续消费助推手段。生成式人工智能可支持大规模生成此类理论一致的解释，并为未来根据场景、用户涉入度和行为目标进行自适应解释设计提供了实践基础。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [131] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究表明：概念瓶颈模型即使能准确预测射血分数，其内部预测的心室容积也可能缺乏真实物理尺度，因此仅以最终任务性能或概念相关性评估可解释性并不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型通常通过可解释的中间变量进行预测，并以中间变量预测准确度作为有效性依据。作者质疑这一标准是否足够，特别是在超声心动图中以左心室舒张末期容积和收缩末期容积为概念、推导射血分数的场景下。

Method: 研究使用公开超声心动图视频数据集训练视频Transformer编码器，将舒张末期容积和收缩末期容积作为概念层，并通过解析公式计算射血分数，且不设置绕过概念层的残差预测路径。作者比较了仅优化射血分数损失与同时额外监督容积毫升值的两种训练方式，并在1276项留出检查上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈模型的射血分数误差未劣于直接回归模型，平均绝对误差分别为6.89和7.13。仅使用射血分数监督时，预测容积的离散程度塌缩至0.1毫升，远低于参考容积的35.7和45.7毫升，尽管相关性仍部分保留。原因在于射血分数是容积比值：两个容积同时按相同比例缩放不会改变射血分数，因而该损失无法确定概念层的绝对尺度。加入绝对容积监督后，容积误差从89.8毫升降至25.8毫升，但射血分数误差增加0.4。

Conclusion: 最终预测准确并不保证概念变量具有正确且可解释的物理含义。对于由存在不变性的目标函数训练的概念瓶颈模型，应检验概念变量是否被目标函数唯一约束，并采用绝对单位误差、分布尺度及物理一致性等指标验证其有效性，而不能仅依赖预测性能或相关性。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [132] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“自推测智能体”框架，使同一大语言模型同时承担任务执行与下一工具调用预测，通过复用前缀KV缓存来隐藏工具调用等待延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用常导致智能体长时间等待。现有推测器通常是独立草稿模型或缓存轨迹，与实际部署智能体的行为存在预测偏差，即“推测器—智能体鸿沟”。作者发现目标智能体自身就是较强的下一调用预测器，因此尝试将两种角色统一。

Method: 方法采用单模型双模式设计：在智能体模式下完成任务，在推测器模式下根据部分轨迹预测下一次工具调用；二者共享模型参数并完全复用前缀KV缓存。训练上提出联合智能体—推测器强化学习：从智能体自身rollout中构造推测目标，并交替执行智能体更新和推测器更新，以避免预测能力训练损害原有任务能力。

Result: 在智能体搜索问答和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B的平均下一工具调用Hit@1由44.1提升至61.2；Qwen3.5-4B由48.9提升至66.3。同时，智能体的任务成功率得以保持。

Conclusion: 将执行智能体与工具调用推测器合并为同一模型，可显著提高下一工具调用预测准确率，并在不降低任务表现的前提下通过预执行工具调用减少端到端等待时间。该方案还避免了额外独立推测模型带来的行为失配与部署复杂度。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [133] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文面向大规模分布式约束优化问题，结合在线学习与分解优化框架，在去中心化卫星调度实例上实现接近最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DCOP方法在通信受限环境下适用于分布式决策，但面对大规模实际问题时，整体求解的计算复杂度和协调开销过高。论文旨在同时提升不完全求解算法的效率，以及大规模任务分配与局部调度的可扩展性。

Method: 首先，论文重新研究DCOP与势博弈之间的联系，将用于寻找均衡的现代在线学习算法改造为DCOP求解方法，并与代表性不完全DCOP算法比较。其次，提出两层分解框架：上层元DCOP负责任务分配，下层由相互独立的局部优化器负责调度。为协调两层决策，设计迭代定价机制，根据局部调度器的反馈持续修正上层任务效用；最终将在线学习方法嵌入该分解与定价框架。

Result: 在线学习型DCOP算法在性能上能够与代表性不完全DCOP算法竞争。结合迭代定价的两层框架在真实去中心化卫星调度问题上达到近似最优结果，完成超过99%的观测请求，而现有先进基线方法约完成87%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，势博弈视角下的在线学习可作为有效的不完全DCOP求解工具；通过将全局任务分配与局部调度解耦，并利用反馈驱动的迭代定价进行耦合协调，可显著提升大规模分布式优化问题的可扩展性和实际效能。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [134] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出HiSkill：一种面向LLM智能体的分层技能图框架，将高层可复用技能与可执行动作模板连接起来，并通过任务相关子图检索和子图引导执行，提升长程交互任务的性能与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从轨迹中提取技能的方法通常形成彼此独立、扁平化的文本技能集合，未充分利用技能之间的分解、时序和依赖关系；同时，高层自然语言技能与实际可执行动作之间存在落地鸿沟，导致检索、规划和执行的效率受限。

Method: HiSkill将交互轨迹组织为有向分层图，包含技能节点（Skill）、原子操作节点（AtomicOp）及类型化边。该图一方面以原子操作作为可执行动作模板，连接高层技能与具体动作；另一方面编码技能间的分解、时间转移、兼容、支持和恢复等关系。推理时，系统先检索紧凑的任务相关子图，再结合符号化任务状态、当前激活技能和该子图，迭代决定技能切换、原子操作选择及动作参数落地。

Result: 在三个交互式环境上的实验表明，HiSkill优于现有最先进基线方法；同时，由于只检索并使用紧凑的相关子图，其推理阶段的token消耗得到降低。摘要未提供具体环境名称、性能数值、统计显著性或消融实验细节。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，使用分层技能图显式建模高层技能、原子操作及其多类型关系，能够有效缩小抽象技能与动作执行之间的差距。其核心贡献在于将结构化技能关系用于检索和执行控制，从而兼顾长程任务的可复用性、执行可靠性与推理成本；但其泛化能力、图构建成本和不同关系类型的独立贡献仍需结合全文实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [135] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向精算风险建模的大语言模型多智能体框架，通过校准后的任务级置信度与贝叶斯网络实现工作流不确定性传播，在保持基准精算性能的同时增强稳定性与运行时风险分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流属于高风险决策支持场景，大语言模型的概率性输出及多智能体之间的依赖关系可能造成风险评估偏差、定价不公或合规问题。因此，需要一种能够量化并传播任务不确定性的方法，以提升多智能体系统在精算应用中的可靠性和可审计性。

Method: 框架以中央枢纽协调多个专用智能体，分别负责数据准备、建模、审查和解释。方法首先从模型输出中提取词元级对数概率，并进行长度归一化汇总；随后将这些汇总值转换为经过校准的任务级置信度，而非直接将对数概率解释为结果正确率或任务成功概率；最后，利用贝叶斯网络表达智能体任务之间的条件依赖关系，并执行全流程的不确定性传播。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够复现基准精算模型的性能，同时提供额外的信息，包括各工作流环节的稳定性、任务依赖导致的不确定性变化，以及运行时端到端不确定性传播情况。

Conclusion: 将词元级对数概率经校准后作为任务级置信度，并结合贝叶斯网络建模多智能体依赖关系，是在不牺牲精算建模性能的前提下提升大语言模型多智能体系统可靠性的重要方案。该方法尤其适用于需要透明风险评估、过程监控和不确定性解释的精算工作流。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [136] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了SHarD（Secure Harness Distribution），一种可分发的AI编程代理安全加固框架。实验表明，通过单条安装命令部署操作系统沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制等控制措施后，SHarD可达到与最佳安全配置商业代理相同的100%调整后得分，且未出现测试类别性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理的快速采用受到安全与风险顾虑制约。现有控制措施通常难以系统化分发给分散的工程团队，而厂商原生方案可能带来生态绑定和部署适配限制。因此，论文关注通用、现成的安全控制能否被封装到自定义代理运行框架中，并以可扩展方式部署到大量用户。

Method: 研究采用分阶段测试方法，对四类配置进行比较：两种商业AI代理在启用与未启用安全控制时的表现、基线代理框架，以及安全加固后的SHarD框架。评估使用由OWASP Agentic Applications Top 10构建的23项测试套件，重点验证三类控制：操作系统级沙箱、技能/插件扫描、工具调用限制。SHarD构建于Pi agent harness之上，并通过统一安装机制实现控制措施分发。

Result: SHarD在调整后评分中达到100%，与安全配置最佳的商业代理持平；在全部测试类别中均未出现回归。结果支持以下结论：安全控制可以不依赖特定厂商原生机制，而通过自定义代理框架实现统一封装和分发；操作系统沙箱能够有效缓解自主代理跨越系统边界的风险；模型输出和代理行为的非确定性可能导致安全结果不稳定，因此安全评估需要考虑重复试验和波动性。

Conclusion: 论文证明，面向AI编程代理的安全控制能够以可安装、可分发的框架形式规模化交付，并在测试中保持不低于商业代理直接部署控制时的防护效果。SHarD为降低厂商依赖、统一企业内部代理安全基线提供了可行路径。论文同时提出了控制框架适配性评估的初步特征，并将更完整的框架适配性研究列为后续工作。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [137] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier 是一个统一的 AI 智能体评测语料库，汇集近百万条标准化评测记录，用于比较不同模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器与计分规则下的能力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测存在任务、执行框架、验证机制和评分规则割裂的问题；许多基准覆盖范围狭窄、规模有限，或复现实验成本高，导致已有实证结果难以横向比较与系统审计。

Method: 作者构建 Messier 语料库，包含 957,253 条记录，覆盖 30 个基准、714 个智能体、11,891 个任务及 74,205 个验证器。数据整合公开基准分数，并补充五个智能体在六个代表不足的专业与科学领域中的运行结果。每条记录按模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则标准化，同时引入 SOC/NAICS 分类以支持职业与行业维度分析；作者还通过反事实重新评分检验聚合规则对结果的影响，并据此构建能力尺度。

Result: 分析显示，前沿智能体的进步在不同基准类型上并不均衡：函数调用任务趋于饱和，编程任务提升最快，企业工作流最具挑战性。反事实评分表明，多验证器任务中的严格“全通过”聚合规则可能掩盖真实进步，并人为改变智能体排名。基于标准化记录得到的能力尺度与 Epoch Evaluation Capability Index 的排名具有较高一致性，Spearman 相关系数为 0.81，且可按领域、职业、动作空间和验证器类型进行专门化。

Conclusion: Messier 为智能体能力评测提供了可复用的数据基础设施，使跨基准比较、能力尺度构建、评测审计及失败模式的细粒度分析成为可能。论文的核心贡献在于揭示评分聚合规则会显著影响能力结论，并通过大规模统一记录提高了评测结果的可比性与可解释性。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [138] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出交互式奖励代理IRA，用于评估GUI智能体是否完成任务；其通过“提出完成条件—调用工具验证证据”的机制结合界面与环境状态，在GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%准确率，并可为GUI智能体训练提供有效奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据执行截图难以可靠判断GUI任务是否完成，因为许多任务依赖不可见的系统配置、文件内容或应用设置。现有自动评估器因缺少对执行后环境状态的访问，难以生成准确且可用于训练的奖励信号。

Method: IRA接收任务指令及智能体执行后的GUI环境，先推断任务成功所需的可验证条件，再通过系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具获取证据并逐项核验。论文还构建GUI-RewardBench，包含321条任务轨迹，覆盖Ubuntu桌面环境中的10类应用，用于比较不同GUI任务评估方法。

Result: IRA在GUI-RewardBench上取得86.9%的评估准确率，优于已有评估基线。将IRA生成的奖励用于GUI智能体强化学习后，智能体在OSWorld上的任务成功率达到34.0%，表明其奖励信号具有实际训练价值。

Conclusion: 该工作将GUI任务评估从基于截图的静态判断扩展为对执行后环境的交互式证据验证，显著提高了评估可靠性。其核心贡献是提出可操作的奖励代理框架与基准数据集，并证明高质量自动评估能够有效支持GUI智能体的后训练优化。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [139] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Penelope：一种面向预训练仅解码器Transformer的高效隐式推理框架。其核心是在解码器的局部层区间中执行循环式潜变量细化，以较低延迟获得接近现有隐式推理方法的结构化推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理通常依赖增大模型参数量或输出长链式思维文本。前者提高训练与部署成本，后者使计算量受自回归生成长度约束，并需要反复执行完整解码器。论文旨在将额外推理计算转移到内部潜空间，避免长中间文本及全模型重复计算。

Method: Penelope先仅执行一次较低层的解码器前缀，生成与输入问题相关的边界记忆；随后只在选定的解码器局部区间内进行迭代计算。该过程采用时间调制GRU动态机制与循环读出状态，对潜在表示持续细化；最后再进行答案生成。训练上使用从显式链式思维到隐式潜变量推理的渐进式课程，将可见推理过程迁移至内部循环路径。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准上，Penelope在经验证集选择的潜变量计算预算下，达到与既有隐式推理模型具有竞争力的准确率，同时降低了实测推理延迟。结果表明，额外推理不必反复运行完整解码器，也不必生成很长的显式中间推理文本。

Conclusion: 论文证明了局部化潜变量细化是仅解码器Transformer中可行的推理扩展方式。其主要价值在于通过“单次前缀计算＋局部循环细化”提供准确率与效率之间的实用折中；但摘要未给出具体模型规模、基准名称、延迟降幅及不同计算预算下的详细性能，因此其实际收益仍需结合完整实验进一步评估。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [140] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出对dtControl2的扩展，通过允许可控误差ε，将马尔可夫决策过程中的控制器压缩为更小、更易理解的决策树，同时保证ε-最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树虽可解释，但面对大规模系统或大量边界情形时仍会变得复杂。直接删减树节点可能遗漏关键状态，从而使控制器失效，因此需要一种具有性能保证的简化方法。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程设定下，引入ε功能：用户指定允许的不精确度ε≥0，工具在该误差预算内构造较小的决策树，用以近似原控制器，并形式化保证所得策略满足ε-最优性。

Result: 实验表明，该工具生成的决策树相较现有方法可缩小多个数量级，同时保留可调节的性能近似保证。

Conclusion: 该方法将控制器解释的复杂度与性能损失建立了可控权衡：增大允许误差可获得更简洁的决策树，从而在保证近似最优的前提下提炼控制器的核心决策逻辑。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [141] [A Cost-Effective Multimodal LLM Reasoning Framework for Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25947)
*Frank Nie,Ethan B Liu,Yuan Zhu,Wei Fan,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinPRISM是一种面向不规则临床时间序列问答的多模态大语言模型框架，通过多尺度建模、证据压缩和渐进式对齐，在较低推理成本下实现了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列问答模型难以有效处理临床观测中常见的稀疏性、异步性与不规则采样问题，导致其对跨时间尺度临床证据的提取和推理能力受限。

Method: 方法包含三项核心设计：其一，采用不规则性感知的多尺度编码器，从不同时间粒度提取稀疏临床证据；其二，使用时间证据蒸馏器融合多尺度表示，并将其压缩为少量可被大语言模型处理的时间序列令牌；其三，通过渐进式对齐策略，逐步将不规则轨迹表示映射到语言模型的文本嵌入空间。训练数据包括3万条带多尺度描述的临床时间序列，以及覆盖11项任务的4.1万条指令微调样本。

Result: 基于40亿参数语言模型骨干网络，ClinPRISM在留出评测基准上取得了最优性能；模型每个样本仅使用16个时间序列令牌，单问题平均推理延迟为0.15秒，表明其具有较好的表示效率和部署可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，通过针对不规则采样特征设计编码、跨尺度证据融合和表示对齐机制，可以显著提升大语言模型在临床时间序列问答中的效果，同时避免依赖大量时间序列令牌，从而兼顾准确性与计算效率。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over irregular clinical time series (ICTS) plays a pivotal role in a wide range of healthcare applications. Although recent multimodal time-series large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in general-purpose time-series QA, they remain poorly equipped to model the sparsity, asynchrony, and irregular sampling patterns of clinical observations. To fill this gap, we propose ClinPRISM, a cost-effective multimodal LLM reasoning framework for question answering over ICTS data. First, we devise an irregularity-aware multi-scale encoder to capture sparse clinical evidence at diverse temporal scales. Then, we propose a temporal evidence distiller to integrate representations across these scales and compress them into a small number of LLM-compatible tokens. Moreover, we introduce a progressive alignment strategy that sequentially aligns the irregular trajectories with the LLM's textual embedding space. To facilitate training, we construct 30,000 clinical time series paired with multi-scale descriptions, together with 41,000 instruction-tuning instances spanning 11 tasks. Using a 4-billion-parameter LLM backbone, ClinPRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the held-out evaluation benchmark while using only 16 time-series tokens and achieving an average inference latency of 0.15 seconds per question.

</details>


### [142] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多仓库存分配的实例级MIP建模选择框架：利用大语言模型在多个候选运筹优化专家公式之间进行选择，并通过求解器反馈、偏好优化与强化学习提升选择质量。实验显示，该方法优于固定公式和仅经SFT+IPO训练的选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库存分配受需求集中度、库存失衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动等因素影响，不同实例适合不同的MIP公式。单一固定建模方式难以稳定适应异质性场景，因此需要根据具体实例自动选择最合适的优化公式。

Method: 方法将每个候选MIP公式视为一个具有不同分配优先级的“OR专家”。首先构造类别均衡、以专家为条件的监督微调数据，使模型学习实例模式与公式选择之间的对应关系；随后在历史实例上运行MIP求解器，将不同专家的实际分配质量差距转化为带间隔权重的IPO偏好数据。最后，利用每个实例的专家得分元数据建立奖励查找机制，并以GRPO对模型采样的专家选择进行强化学习优化。

Result: 在企业真实多仓库存分配数据上的实验表明，GRPO显著提升专家选择表现：Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。最终选择器相较既有基线获得12.57个百分点的分配准确率增益，优于SFT+IPO选择器及最佳固定OR专家；其与事后最优预言机之间的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 论文说明，基于求解器结果训练的LLM可以有效执行实例级运筹优化公式选择。相对于仅依赖监督学习或偏好学习的方案，结合GRPO和实例级奖励反馈能更准确地识别适宜的MIP公式，并转化为更高的实际库存分配质量。该研究的核心贡献是将LLM训练信号与优化求解器评价直接耦合，用于动态选择而非生成运筹公式。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [143] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CH​​ARM，一种面向多模态图零样本迁移的图基础模型，通过分层图上下文建模和大语言模型增强跨领域、跨模态知识迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图数据中的节点通常包含文本、图像等多种模态，而为每个新领域收集标注并进行模型适配成本高且不可行。现有GNN图基础模型往往依赖下游微调，基于大语言模型的方法又多局限于单模态图或单一领域任务，因此多模态图的零样本迁移仍缺乏有效方案。核心困难在于：既要学习各模态的可迁移知识及其跨模态关系，又要避免节点表示被目标领域特有的图结构和模态特征所束缚。

Method: CH​​ARM以分层图上下文替代孤立的原始节点表示。该上下文同时编码节点周边的图结构、多模态语义信息及跨模态关系，并将领域特定的节点模式抽象映射为可跨领域共享的高层概念。模型采用模态感知的图上下文编码器融合不同模态与图拓扑，随后将生成的表示转换为图令牌（graph tokens），输入大语言模型进行建模和推理。该设计旨在降低对目标领域标签、微调和适配过程的依赖。

Result: 实验表明，CH​​ARM在零样本多模态图任务上取得了稳定提升。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标、对比方法名称或数值结果，因此无法进一步量化其相对性能增幅。

Conclusion: CH​​ARM通过“分层上下文抽象—模态感知图编码—图令牌结合大语言模型”的框架，缓解了多模态图零样本迁移中的领域特异性和模态特异性表示耦合问题。其主要贡献是将跨模态关系与高层共享概念纳入图基础模型，从而提升模型在未见图领域上的直接泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过模拟人工智能技术竞赛的重复博弈实验发现，不安全开发行为主要并非由个体风险偏好驱动，而是受竞争中的相对位置、对手行为和早期决策惯性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术竞赛可能使参与者为了抢占进度而牺牲安全性。研究旨在检验不同程度的开发风险是否会改变不安全行为，并识别风险偏好与竞争性动态在此类行为中的相对作用。

Method: 研究采用预注册的行为实验：两名参与者在不确定持续时间的竞赛中，反复选择“安全”或“不安全”开发。不安全选择可带来更快进度和即时收益，但会累积私人风险；实验设置了10%、60%和90%三种最高风险上限，同时保持竞赛结构不变。研究进一步基于重复互动数据进行探索性分析，并构建含四类策略的简化演化模型：始终安全、始终不安全、条件性安全和条件性反社会安全。

Result: 预注册分析未支持最高风险水平之间存在显著差异，也未发现个体风险偏好能够有效解释不安全选择。探索性结果表明：对手上一轮选择不安全会提高个体随后选择不安全的概率；处于领先地位会减少不安全行为，落后则会增加该行为；首轮选择对后续行为具有预测力。演化模型能够复现实验中的处理效应，并说明竞争环境可使条件性不安全策略获得优势。

Conclusion: 不安全开发并不只是个人风险偏好的结果，更可能源于竞赛压力下的相对落后恐惧、对手行为反馈及早期行为形成的路径依赖。因此，促进安全发展的政策应降低过度竞争压力、强化合作与协调机制，而不应仅聚焦于改变个体的风险态度。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


### [145] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Desktop-Delta Bench（DDB），用于评估桌面GUI智能体是否能正确识别一次操作导致的因果性界面状态变化，而非仅判断任务最终是否成功或进行单帧定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体评测主要关注端到端任务成功率和单帧视觉定位，缺少对“动作后界面是否发生了预期变化”的诊断。由于输入、应用渲染和截图采集存在异步性，智能体可能将延迟、遮挡、短暂或无关的画面变化误判为操作进展，进而导致后续规划错误、状态陈旧或恢复失败。

Method: DDB构建了一个离线、步骤级数据集，包含2,013个经人工验证的样例，来源于跨约15类Linux应用、50个任务领域的新型多应用轨迹。基准覆盖状态验证、变化来源追踪和上下文感知控制三类失效维度，并设置两项互补任务：其一为463个三帧时序排序样例，其中105个包含来自其他轨迹的干扰帧；其二为1,550个操作前后图像对，标注5类动作及其对应载荷。作者在32种排序设置和16种单动作设置下评估了8个闭源与开源模型家族。

Result: 时序排序任务尚未饱和：最佳模型在无干扰样例和含干扰样例上的精确匹配率分别为65.1%和65.7%。加入任务上下文可将干扰帧识别提升6.9个百分点，但会使无干扰排序精确匹配率下降2.2个百分点；错误分析显示模型存在机械复述输入A-B-C呈现顺序的系统性偏差。单动作任务表明，推断动作类别通常比定位动作位置更困难：点击动作的F1为0.96，而拖拽动作仅为0.76；但当模型已正确识别拖拽时，其位置定位通常较准确。

Conclusion: DDB补足了GUI单帧感知与端到端任务成功之间缺失的诊断层，可用于定位桌面智能体在状态确认、异步变化归因、干扰排除和失败恢复中的具体弱点。结果说明，现有模型虽具备一定界面定位能力，但对动作—状态变化的时序因果理解仍不足，后续应重点提升上下文约束下的变化验证与动作类型判别能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Kernel-Checked Exclusions for the Erdős-Selfridge Odd Covering Problem: Any Odd Covering of $\mathbb{Z}$ Has lcm Exceeding 10000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25628)
*Ibrahim Mian,Shayaan Siddique*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文在 Lean 4 中端到端形式化验证了 Erdős-Selfridge 奇模覆盖问题的一项有限范围排除结论：任何由互异奇数模数（均大于 1）构成的整数覆盖系统，其模数最小公倍数必大于 10000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题尚未解决，已有数学论证与更大范围的非认证分类，但缺少可由证明内核完全检查的结果。本文目标不是提出新的核心数学机制，而是将已有排除性知识转化为具备高可信度、可复用且可持续验证的形式化定理。

Method: 证明结合四部分：其一，形式化密度论证，导出若模数均整除 N 的覆盖存在，则 2N≤σ₁(N)，即 N 必为完全数或丰盈数；其二，内核验证奇数 N<945 均不满足该丰盈条件；其三，对所有小于 10^4 的 23 个奇数丰盈候选，构造基于中国剩余定理的容量证书，以可判定算术不等式逐一排除覆盖；其四，内核验证枚举结果，确认这 23 个数是该范围内全部非亏数候选。论文还建立了整数上的覆盖与模 N 剩余类有限检查之间的双向周期性桥梁，并将结果接入 formal-conjectures 中的 StrictCoveringSystem ℤ 表述。

Result: Lean 内核检查了 63 个已发布定理，证明不存在最小公倍数不超过 10000 的此类覆盖系统。形式化证明不使用 sorry、native_decide 或受信任基中的外部求解器；所有定理仅依赖 propext、Classical.choice 和 Quot.sound 三项公理。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是认识论和工程层面的：它将一个已知有限排除结果提升为可由 Lean 证明内核独立核验的定理，并通过 CI 中的公理依赖门控保障可信边界。其周期性有限化框架也为未来接入 SAT 风格搜索所产生的证书提供了形式化验证接口。

Abstract: The Erdős-Selfridge odd covering problem (Erdős problem #7) asks whether a covering system of $\mathbb{Z}$ exists whose moduli are all odd, distinct, and greater than 1. The problem is open. We present a Lean 4 formalization, checked end to end by the proof kernel, of the exclusion: any covering of $\mathbb{Z}$ by finitely many congruence classes with distinct odd moduli > 1 has lcm of the moduli exceeding 10000. The proof composes a formalized density argument (a covering by divisors of $N$ exceeding 1 forces $2N \le σ_1(N)$, so the lcm is abundant or perfect), a kernel-checked abundancy floor (no odd $N < 945$ qualifies), a family of Chinese-Remainder capacity certificates -- decidable per-$N$ arithmetic inequalities each refuting every covering with distinct moduli > 1 dividing that $N$ -- for all 23 odd abundant numbers below $10^4$, and a kernel-checked enumeration establishing that those 23 are the only odd non-deficient candidates. The result is transported to the official StrictCoveringSystem $\mathbb{Z}$ formulation of Erdős #7 in google-deepmind/formal-conjectures, with a bidirectional periodicity bridge between coverings of $\mathbb{Z}$ and finite checks over $\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}$ suitable for consuming future SAT-style search output. All 63 published theorems depend on exactly propext, Classical.choice, and Quot.sound: no sorry, no native_decide, no solver in the trusted base. The mathematical content is known -- the density argument is folklore, and far larger uncertified classifications of covering numbers exist -- so the contribution is epistemic rather than mathematical: these exclusions are theorems of the Lean kernel, with an axiom gate enforced mechanically in continuous integration.

</details>


### [147] [Verification of Provers and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25793)
*René Thiemann*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文综述了将自动演绎工具与证明助手连接的方法，重点比较认证与验证两类集成策略，并概述其成功应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明器、SAT/SMT 求解器和终止性分析器具有高自动化效率，但其结果需要在证明助手的可信内核框架中获得可靠保证，因此需要研究二者的连接机制。

Method: 文章采用综述与比较的方法，梳理自动演绎工具接入证明助手的不同途径，重点讨论认证（由外部工具产生可检查的证明或证书）和验证（在证明助手中验证外部工具结果）策略，并列举应用案例。

Result: 章节指出，不同连接方案在可信性、实现复杂度、性能和适用工具类型方面存在权衡；认证和验证机制能够使证明助手利用外部自动化工具，同时维持结果的可检验性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 自动演绎工具与证明助手的有效集成可兼顾自动化能力和形式化可信性。选择认证或验证方案时，应结合目标工具、证明对象、性能需求及可信计算基范围进行权衡。

Abstract: Automatic deduction tools such as automatic theorem provers, SAT (satisfiability) solvers, SMT (satisfiability modulo theories) solvers, and termination analyzers can be connected to proof assistants using various approaches, notably by certification and verification. This chapter reviews and compares the approaches available, and mentions several successful applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Beyond "What to Retrieve": Uncertainty in Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24884)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Xiaoli Lian,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenCoder 是一种面向仓库级代码生成的不确定性感知框架，通过估计不同证据源的不确定性来筛选、排序并指导生成、验证与修复。其在部分模型设置下显著优于基础检索增强生成，但收益具有后端依赖性，且与验证修复基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需要综合相似代码、仓库上下文和项目 API 等异构证据。这些证据虽然互补，却可能包含噪声、冗余或冲突。现有检索增强方法主要关注相关性，未显式考虑检索证据的不确定性及其对后续代码生成质量的影响。

Method: 作者提出 OpenCoder：首先针对不同来源的证据估计源特异性不确定性；随后利用该不确定性进行异构证据过滤与排序；最后将其作为控制信号用于代码生成、结果验证和错误修复。实验还对 API 知识、仓库上下文和相似代码三类信息进行了析因分析，并评估目标感知的 API 精炼对 API 集检索的作用。

Result: 析因分析表明，不存在对所有条件均有效的证据源固定叠加排序；不同证据源之间存在显著交互，其效果取决于同时提供的证据及所使用的大语言模型后端。在扩展后的 32 个 RepoExec-inline 任务上，OpenCoder 将 GPT 的选定输出正确率从 Baseline RAG 的 56.25% 提升至 78.13%。但该方法与验证修复对照方法表现相当；对于 Gemini，提升缺乏统计支持。目标感知 API 精炼则显著提升了 API 集检索效果。

Conclusion: 不确定性可以作为仓库级代码生成中的有效控制信号，用于改进证据检索、生成后的验证和修复流程。但 OpenCoder 的端到端收益并非普适：其相对优势受模型后端影响，并且部分提升可能可由验证与修复机制本身解释。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation relies on heterogeneous evidence whose relevance, compatibility, and completeness are inherently uncertain. Similar-code examples, repository context, and project-specific APIs may provide complementary information, but can also introduce noisy, redundant, or conflicting signals. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches primarily optimize retrieval relevance without explicitly modeling how uncertainty in retrieved evidence affects downstream generation. We introduce OpenCoder, an uncertainty-aware framework that estimates source-specific uncertainty, uses it to filter and rank heterogeneous evidence, and guides generation, verification, and repair. A factorial analysis over API knowledge, repository context, and similar-code evidence reveals no universal additive source ranking; instead, significant cross-source interactions depend on the accompanying evidence and LLM backend. On an expanded 32-task RepoExec-inline evaluation, OpenCoder improves GPT selected-output correctness over Baseline RAG from 56.25\% to 78.13\%. However, it matches a verification-and-repair control, and the corresponding Gemini improvement is not statistically supported, indicating backend-dependent benefits. Target-aware API refinement also substantially improves API-set retrieval. These findings support treating uncertainty as an actionable control signal for repository-level retrieval, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [149] [Characterizing Structural Testability in JavaScript: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24965)
*Shahrzad Mirzaei,Saba Alimadadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向现代 JavaScript 系统的结构可测试性评估框架，通过七个静态结构维度构建综合可测试性评分（CTS），并在30个开源项目上进行实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可测试性研究主要聚焦面向对象和并发软件，难以充分描述 JavaScript 中异步执行、事件驱动控制流、闭包及动态交互带来的测试挑战。因此，论文旨在系统刻画 JavaScript 软件的结构可测试性及其分布特征。

Method: 研究将结构可测试性定义为七维构念：可控性、可观测性、分支复杂度、异步协调、事件驱动行为、封装性和副作用强度。作者基于抽象语法树静态分析提取这些维度的指标，并聚合形成 CTS；随后在30个不同规模和领域的开源 JavaScript 项目中，于函数、文件和项目层面开展比较分析、低分函数结构模式识别及项目特征关联分析。

Result: 结果表明，结构上难以测试的函数并非均匀分布，而是集中于少量文件中；低 CTS 函数存在多种重复出现的结构原型，说明低可测试性并不由单一代码模式主导，而是可由不同的异步、事件、分支、封装和副作用组合共同造成。

Conclusion: 论文为 JavaScript 结构可测试性的度量提供了可操作的静态分析框架和大规模经验依据。CTS 可用于识别测试风险集中的代码区域，并为后续研究测试工作量预测、自动化测试生成、面向可测试性的重构及软件质量评估提供基础。

Abstract: Software testability has long been recognized as a software quality attribute that influences testing effort and effectiveness. While prior work has extensively studied testability in object-oriented and concurrent software, comparatively little is known about structural testability in modern JavaScript systems. JavaScript applications rely on asynchronous execution, event-driven control flow, closures, and dynamic interactions that are not explicitly captured by existing testability frameworks. This paper presents a large-scale empirical study of structural testability in JavaScript. We operationalize structural testability as a seven-dimensional construct capturing controllability, observability, branching complexity, asynchronous coordination, event-driven behaviour, encapsulation, and side-effect intensity. These dimensions are derived from AST-based static analysis and aggregated into a Composite Testability Score (CTS) for comparative analysis across functions, files, and projects. We apply this framework to 30 open-source JavaScript projects spanning diverse domains and sizes. Our analysis characterizes the distribution of structural testability, identifies recurring structural archetypes among low-CTS functions, and examines associations between project characteristics and testability. We find that structurally-challenging functions are concentrated within a relatively small subset of files and arise through multiple recurring structural configurations rather than a single dominant pattern. These findings provide new insight into structural testability in JavaScript and establish a foundation for future research on testing effort, automated test generation, testability-aware refactoring, and software quality assessment.

</details>


### [150] [Preliminary Guidelines for Using and Evaluating GenAI Tools to Support Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24991)
*Barbara Kitchenham,Sebastián Pizard,Lech Madeyski,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Martin Shepperd,David Budgen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出GUEST（GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks）流程建议，用于指导在系统文献综述（SLR）中使用和评估生成式人工智能及大语言模型。核心观点是：现阶段GenAI不能在无人监督下独立完成严谨的系统综述，但可在人工监督下为重复性工作和部分复杂任务的验证提供具成本效益的辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能和大语言模型已被用于软件工程及其他领域的学术任务，包括文献总结、筛选和证据提取等SLR环节。然而，SLR要求严格的方法学规范、结果可靠性、过程透明度与可复现性，而模型输出可能存在不稳定、不可解释、难以验证或无法完整追溯等问题。论文旨在为两类研究者提供指导：一是计划借助GenAI实施SLR的研究者，二是评估GenAI对SLR任务支持效果的实证研究者。

Method: 研究采用两阶段方法。第一阶段开展快速综述，识别已有关于使用或评估GenAI与大语言模型支持SLR的指南性研究。第二阶段结合思想实验、相关文献中的方法指导，以及作者开展SLR和评估工具的实践经验，归纳在SLR场景下使用与评价GenAI时应遵循的流程性建议。分析覆盖SLR的规划、实施和报告阶段，也覆盖GenAI工具独立评估研究的设计与报告问题。

Result: 论文识别了评估GenAI支持SLR时面临的关键问题，并系统讨论了研究者在规划、执行和报告“使用GenAI的SLR”及“评估GenAI工具的研究”时需要处理的过程问题。最终将发现凝练为GUEST建议集。该框架强调明确任务边界和人机分工、保留过程记录与决策依据、建立人工核验与质量控制机制、采用适当基准和评价标准，以及透明报告模型、提示、参数、版本、数据与验证过程等信息，以提升研究的可信度与可复现性。

Conclusion: 论文认为，GenAI当前不具备无监督完成系统性研究的能力，人工监督、审查和最终责任不可替代。但对于重复性任务，或作为复杂任务的额外验证来源，GenAI具有降低成本和提升效率的潜力。GUEST可帮助软件工程研究者更可信地开展和报告使用GenAI的SLR，并支持对相关工具进行更严格、独立且透明的实证评估。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI (GenAI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for academic tasks in software engineering and beyond, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). However, while capable of summarizing text, there is no guarantee they can meet the rigour, reliability, and transparency that SLRs require. Objectives: To support researchers intending to conduct SLRs using GenAI or those conducting empirical studies evaluating how well GenAI supports SLR tasks. Methods: First, we conducted a rapid review to identify studies that propose guidelines for evaluating and using GenAI and LLMs to support SLRs. Second, we drew on thought experiments, relevant guidance from the literature, and our own experience conducting SLRs and evaluating tools to develop recommendations for how to use and assess GenAI in the context of SLRs. Results: We discuss the problems researchers face when evaluating GenAI for SLRs. We identify and explain process issues to consider when planning, conducting, and reporting both SLRs using GenAI and evaluations of GenAI tools. Finally, we summarize our results as a set of process recommendations, which we name GUEST (GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks). Conclusion: We argue that GenAI requires human oversight and is not currently capable of unsupervised systematic studies. However, it offers the prospect of cost-effective assistance for some repetitive tasks and for additional validation of some complex tasks. Our GUEST recommendations should help software engineering researchers both to conduct and report trustworthy SLRs using GenAI and to provide rigorous independent evaluation studies.

</details>


### [151] [Authoring Agent Skills: A Software-Engineering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25032)
*Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出应将 Agent Skills 视为可复用的软件工件，而非单纯提示词，并以软件工程原则、分阶段加载、行为评估和信任管理指导其设计与使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 用于向大语言模型智能体按需提供程序性知识，但其开放格式和跨工具支持带来了设计质量、上下文令牌预算、运行可靠性、机制选择及第三方技能安全性等问题。作者旨在建立一套系统化的技能编写与评估框架。

Method: 文章以 Claude Code 为参考实现，分析技能的文件结构、分阶段加载机制以及决定技能被选择的描述文本写法；将技能与项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子智能体、外部工具连接和钩子进行比较，并按“由谁决定运行”及“可提供何种保证”给出选择规则。随后提出以评估驱动的编写流程，归纳常见设计模式与故障模式，并通过 UML 类图式比较、加载模型、技能解剖图及会话时序图说明相关概念。

Result: 文章认为高质量技能应遵循单一职责、接口与实现分离、低耦合及共享令牌预算节约等原则。技能选择高度依赖描述文本，内容应支持按需、分层加载；由于智能体输出具有非确定性，传统确定性测试不足，应采用行为评估验证技能效果。不同机制适合不同控制需求：需要自动按上下文触发时适合技能，需要强制执行保证时可使用钩子，而显式调用、任务隔离或能力扩展则分别适合命令、子智能体和外部工具。

Conclusion: Agent Skills 的核心价值在于以可组合、可发现、可按需加载的形式沉淀智能体程序性知识。其工程化成功依赖于清晰边界、精炼接口、有效行为评估和正确的机制选型；对于第三方技能，还必须审查其来源、权限、内容与潜在行为，以控制供应链和执行信任风险。

Abstract: Agent Skills are an emerging way to extend large language model agents with reusable procedural knowledge that the agent loads on demand. Anthropic introduced Agent Skills and published the format as an open specification supported across several agent tools. This note argues that a skill is a software artefact and that its construction should follow software-engineering principles, with qualifications: single responsibility, separation of interface from implementation, low coupling, and economy in a shared token budget, together with behavioural evaluation in place of deterministic testing. Using Claude Code as the reference implementation, it describes how a skill is structured, how its contents are loaded in stages, and how to write the description on which selection depends. It places skills against the other mechanisms a developer can use to shape agent behaviour, like project memory files, slash commands, subagents, external tool connections, and hooks, and gives a rule for choosing between them based on who decides that a mechanism runs and what guarantee it provides. It then sets out an evaluation-driven authoring process, a set of patterns and faults commonly encountered in authoring, and the trust question raised by using skills from third parties. We illustrate the comparison drawn in UML class style, the loading model, the anatomy of a skill, the relative position of each mechanism, and the points at which skills and hooks act during a session.

</details>


### [152] [What Motivates Whom? A Survey of Newcomers to OSS and Experienced OSS Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25126)
*Shashiwadana Nirmani,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Mojtaba Shahin,Xiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过208名软件从业者的在线问卷，分析开源软件贡献者在项目选择上的偏好，以及这些偏好与其动机、人口统计特征和经验水平之间的关系，并提出改进项目推荐系统的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目数量持续增长，但潜在贡献者难以识别适合自身的项目，可能导致入门效率低、参与中断和长期留存不足。因此，需要理解不同贡献者选择项目时关注的因素，尤其是新手与有经验实践者之间的差异。

Method: 研究采用在线问卷调查，收集208名软件从业者的数据；考察年龄、性别和所承担的开源角色等特征与参与动机的关联，分析不同动机下对项目年龄、开发阶段和文档质量等项目特征的偏好，并分别比较开源新手和经验丰富实践者的回答；同时收集受访者对现有项目推荐系统的改进建议。

Result: 人口统计特征及开源角色与贡献动机存在显著关联。贡献者对项目年龄、开发阶段和文档质量等特征的偏好，会随其具体动机而变化。新手与经验丰富的开源实践者在项目选择需求上存在差异。受访者还指出，现有推荐系统应更好地结合个人动机和项目特征来支持项目匹配。

Conclusion: 开源项目推荐与贡献者引导不应采用统一策略，而应依据贡献者的经验水平、参与动机及相关背景特征进行差异化设计。研究可为开源项目维护者优化新手引导、参与体验和留存机制提供依据，也为构建更具个性化和动机感知能力的项目推荐系统提供参考。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) development continues to expand, yet software practitioners often struggle to select suitable projects, leading to inefficient onboarding and disengagement. Understanding how contributors select OSS projects is important for supporting contributors onboarding, engagement, and long-term participation within OSS communities. This study investigates contributors' project-selection preferences in OSS projects and examines how these preferences correlate with contributors' motivations and demographic backgrounds. Through an online survey of 208 practitioners, we found that demographic factors, such as age, gender, and the OSS role they held, significantly correlate with their motivations. Additionally, preferences for project characteristics such as project age, development stage, and documentation quality vary based on specific motivations. Importantly, our findings are presented through a comparative lens, analyzing the responses of newcomers to OSS and experienced OSS practitioners separately to uncover their distinct preferences. Lastly, we explore software practitioners' perspectives on how existing recommendation systems could better support project selection and align with their motivations. By disentangling the unique needs of newcomers to OSS and OSS practitioners, our findings provide insights for researchers, OSS project owners, and software practitioners to improve contributor onboarding, engagement, and retention, while also informing future project recommendation systems and improving the OSS ecosystem.

</details>


### [153] [Learning from 53.6K Real-World Developer Edits of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25130)
*Jenny T. Liang,Mihika Bairathi,Wayne Chi,Ameet Talwalkar,Nishant Subramani,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DECODE数据集，包含来自1,000多名开发者的5.36万条真实IDE内AI生成代码编辑记录，覆盖Python、TypeScript和JavaScript。该数据集用于分析开发者如何修改AI代码，并评测大语言模型预测代码编辑的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型主要依赖公开Git提交训练，但提交记录仅反映最终成功版本，无法保留开发者对AI生成代码进行试错、修正、删除和细粒度调整的真实过程。论文旨在填补高质量、真实代码编辑行为数据不足的问题，并推动以开发者实际工作流为中心的编程助手研究。

Method: 作者构建DECODE数据集，收集IDE环境中开发者接受AI代码补全后的后续编辑轨迹。研究首先对编辑发生的时间、原因和方式进行统计分析；随后将数据集用于代码编辑预测基准，比较经DECODE微调的开源30亿参数模型与前沿大语言模型在预测开发者代码修改任务上的表现。

Result: 分析显示，多数编辑发生在接受AI补全后的前15分钟内；在31%的编辑轨迹中，开发者最终删除了AI生成的补全内容，表明部分补全虽被接受但未能长期保留。模型实验显示，使用DECODE微调后，开源30亿参数模型在代码编辑预测任务上的表现显著优于未针对该类真实编辑数据训练的前沿大语言模型。

Conclusion: 真实、细粒度的IDE编辑轨迹能够揭示Git提交数据无法反映的开发者行为，并可显著提升代码编辑预测能力。未来AI编程助手应更多利用开发者实际修改过程进行训练和评测，从而更准确地适应代码生成后的修正需求，而非仅优化初始代码补全。

Abstract: Imperfections in AI-generated code require that software developers modify the generated code manually, or by re-prompting an AI programming assistant. Manual code edits provide more realistic and granular information on editing behavior than Git commits, which only contain final successful code snippets. Yet, due to a lack of high-quality, realistic code editing data, LLMs are mostly trained on publicly available Git data (e.g., commits). To address this gap, we introduce DECODE (Developer Edits of Code Dataset), a dataset of 53.6K real-world in-IDE code edits of AI-generated code in Python, TypeScript, and JavaScript, sourced from 1K+ developers. First, we demonstrate the utility of DECODE for data analysis, obtaining insights on when, why, and how AI-generated code is edited. We find that most edits occur within the first 15 minutes after accepting an AI completion, resulting in the removal of AI completions in 31% of edit trajectories. Second, we use DECODE to benchmark the ability of LLMs to predict code edits. We find that finetuning on DECODE enables open-source 3B models to perform code edit prediction tasks significantly better than frontier LLMs. We then discuss implications of this work, emphasizing the necessity of developer-centric machine learning approaches for future AI programming assistants.

</details>


### [154] [Specification-Driven DevOps for Multi-Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25141)
*Oleg Grynets,Kyrylo Fursov,Vasyl Lyashkevych,Volodymyr Veres*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估前沿大语言模型在缺少开发者部署文件时，仅依据代码仓库生成多服务应用 Dockerfile 与 Docker Compose 配置的能力。结果表明，模型可实现环境“可运行”，但难以完整还原架构、安全、工作流及生产部署意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码仓库中的源代码、依赖声明和服务配置能够提供部分部署线索，但部署工件通常还承载网络隔离、构建优化、端口暴露策略、开发工作流和跨平台兼容性等隐性需求。研究旨在区分“功能正确性”与“部署意图保真度”，并识别无法从仓库可靠推断的信息。

Method: 研究选取三个技术栈异构的多服务仓库，覆盖 Python、Node.js、.NET、React、Rust、Java、Redis、PostgreSQL 与 MySQL 兼容基础设施。模型在未访问开发者原始部署工件的条件下生成容器化配置；随后通过确定性的端到端 HTTP 预言机制验证运行功能，并通过人工结构比对评估生成配置与预期部署结构之间的一致性。

Result: 三个生成环境均成功达到功能可运行状态，其中一个案例需将 Rust 基础镜像从 1.85 更新至 1.88。模型能够重建服务拓扑、应用端口、基础设施依赖、服务主机名、后台工作进程、隐含代理设置及基于文件的 Docker Secrets 机制。但其持续遗漏网络分段、多阶段构建、依赖层缓存、热更新挂载卷、生产级前端服务方式、严格的后端端口限制以及跨平台构建逻辑。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可有效生成满足基本端到端功能的容器化环境，但“能够运行”不能等同于“符合部署设计意图”。对于网络安全边界、性能与构建优化、开发与生产模式差异、端口策略及平台兼容性等信息，应通过显式部署规范提供。论文据此提出最小显式部署规格，以补足仓库工件中不可稳定推断的部署需求。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software environments from repository artifacts. However, functional executability does not necessarily imply conformity with architectural, security, workflow, and production intent. This study investigates whether a frontier LLM can generate Dockerfiles and Docker Compose configurations for multi-service applications using repository contents without access to developer-authored deployment artifacts. Three heterogeneous repositories combining Python, Node.js, .NET, React, Rust, Java, Redis, PostgreSQL, and MySQL-compatible infrastructure were evaluated using deterministic end-to-end HTTP oracles and manual structural comparison. All three generated environments became functionally operational, although one required a Rust base-image update from version 1.85 to 1.88. The model correctly reconstructed service topology, application ports, infrastructure dependencies, service hostnames, a background worker, hidden proxy configurations, and a file-based Docker secrets mechanism. However, it consistently omitted network segmentation, multi-stage builds, dependency-layer caching, live-reload volumes, production frontend serving, restrictive backend-port policies, and cross-platform build logic. Based on these observations, the study formalizes the distinction between functional correctness and deployment-intent fidelity and analytically derives a minimal explicit deployment specification for information that cannot be reliably inferred from repository artifacts.

</details>


### [155] [NFR-to-Code Traceability in a Blockchain-IoT System: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25325)
*Yifei Wang,Jacky Keung,Xiaoxue Ma,Shijie Zhang,Yishu Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文聚焦非功能需求（NFR）到代码的可追踪性问题，基于真实区块链物联网项目构建人工标注数据集，并比较四种检索方法。结果表明，NFR追踪显著难于功能需求（FR）追踪，其中安全相关NFR最难；核心障碍是代码中的实现证据分散且缺乏明确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求到代码追踪研究、公开基准和方法评估主要面向功能需求，难以充分支持安全、可靠性等质量属性的验证。NFR通常具有抽象性、跨模块性和间接实现等特点，因此需要真实项目数据与针对性分析来揭示其追踪困难。

Method: 作者以一个真实区块链物联网项目为对象，设计跨异构工件的追踪链接标注协议，构建同时包含FR和NFR链接的人工整理子集。随后分析NFR追踪特征，并采用TF-IDF、BM25、LSI和WMD四种代表性信息检索基线，对FR到代码与NFR到代码的检索效果进行比较。

Result: 所有评估基线均显示，FR到代码的追踪效果稳定优于NFR到代码。安全类NFR构成最具挑战性的子集。研究还发现，NFR追踪的主要问题并非需求文本缺失，而是对应的实现线索通常分布在多个代码位置，且难以通过局部、显式的代码证据直接识别。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，直接沿用面向FR的传统文本检索式追踪方法不足以解决NFR追踪问题。后续工作应重点建模跨文件、跨模块的分散实现证据，并结合质量属性知识、架构信息或更丰富的代码语义，以提升尤其是安全类NFR的可追踪性。

Abstract: Requirement-to-Code traceability has been widely studied, yet existing research and public benchmarks remain largely centered on functional requirements (FRs). In contrast, traceability for non-functional requirements (NFRs) remains more difficult and underexplored, which hinders the verification of critical quality concerns such as security and reliability.This paper studies NFR-to-Code traceability based on a real-world blockchain-IoT project. We design an annotation protocol for constructing trace links across heterogeneous artifacts and build a manually curated subset containing both FR and NFR links. Using this subset, we examine the characteristics of NFR traceability and further evaluate four representative retrieval baselines: TF-IDF, BM25, LSI, and WMD. The results show that FR-to-Code tracing is consistently easier than NFR-to-Code tracing, while security-related NFRs are the most difficult subset. They further indicate that the main challenge of NFR traceability lies not in requirement availability, but in implementation evidence that is distributed and not clearly localized in code.

</details>


### [156] [Specula: Scaling formal specifications for autonomous model checking of system code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25333)
*Qian Cheng,Saad Mohammad Rafid Pial,Ruize Tang,Yiming Su,Emilie Ma,Finn Hackett,Ivan Beschastnikh,Yu Huang,Tianyin Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Specula 是一个全自动的智能体系统，利用大语言模型生成 TLA+ 形式化规格，并通过模型检查发现复杂系统代码中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形式化方法依赖专家人工构建规格，应用成本高；现有基于大语言模型的方法又容易出现幻觉、奖励投机及对程序行为理解不足等问题，因此需要一种可自动生成且持续改进高质量形式化规格的系统。

Method: 系统采用基于大语言模型的编码智能体，自主构建两类 TLA+ 工件：描述目标系统正确性的不变式，以及以适当抽象层级刻画实现行为的形式化模型。随后通过自我演化循环反复检查、修正和增强规格，使智能体逐步加深对代码及其运行行为的理解，并利用生成的规格执行模型检查和缺陷定位。

Result: 作者将 Specula 应用于 48 个开源系统项目，报告发现了 249 个缺陷，其中包括许多难以被既有方法发现的深层缺陷。该系统还已被多家公司使用，并以开源项目形式维护。

Conclusion: Specula 表明，结合智能体式大语言模型、TLA+ 建模、迭代式规格改进与模型检查，可以显著降低真实系统代码采用形式化验证的门槛，并提升复杂缺陷发现能力。

Abstract: Specula is a push-button agentic system that generates high-quality formal specifications for large, complex system code and uses the specifications for highly effective model checking and bug finding. Specula employs large language model (LLM) based coding agents to autonomously develop TLA+ specifications, including invariants that describe correctness properties of the target system and formal models that describe the system implementation with the right level of abstractions. Specula is fully autonomous and thus eliminates the barrier of applying formal methods to real-world system code (as in traditional human-centric approaches). Meanwhile, Specula addresses limitations of LLM-driven techniques like reward hacking and hallucinations through self-evolving loops that iteratively improve specification quality by enabling the agents to deepen their understanding of system code and its behaviors. We have used Specula to check 48 open-source system projects; Specula found 249 bugs including many deep bugs that are hard to find by existing approaches. Specula has been used by several companies and is maintained at https://github.com/specula-org/Specula.

</details>


### [157] [CodeNib: A Multi-View Data System for Serving Repository Context to Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25431)
*Zhongming Yu,Hengjia Yu,Boqin Yuan,Shuting Zhao,Yizhao Chen,Aryan Dokania,Mihir Jagtap,Jiayu Chang,Yitong Ma,Yash Jayswal,Wentao Ni,Hejia Zhang,Zhaoling Chen,Gangda Deng,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CodeNib：面向代码智能体的统一仓库上下文服务运行时，在每个提交上构建并复用词法、稠密向量和结构化视图，支持搜索、符号导航及受限上下文获取，并在编辑后维护部分视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体在不断变化的仓库中反复进行搜索、导航和上下文保留，但索引、语言服务与任务局部历史彼此割裂，导致重复发现代码信息，且难以评估仓库上下文生命周期中的质量与成本权衡。

Method: CodeNib 为每个仓库提交预计算多视图索引，将查询结果映射到相对仓库的源码范围；在代码修改后增量维护选定视图；通过统一运行时提供排序检索、符号导航和长度受限的上下文。实验覆盖100个仓库快照，比较增量更新与独立重建的一致性及成本；在静态导航请求上比较实时语言服务与静态视图延迟；并在五个模型上比较上下文策略与 grep/read 基线的轨迹令牌消耗及定位能力。

Result: 在输出与独立重建一致的条件下，图结构更新和向量更新的中位速度分别提高8.7倍和25.4倍。对于1,000个请求中可与规范化实时语言服务位置匹配的静态导航子集（占63%），实时/静态的单请求中位延迟比为4.7倍。相较配对的 grep/read 策略，所选上下文策略在保持定位能力的同时，可减少50%至87%的轨迹令牌。

Conclusion: 多视图、可复用的仓库上下文服务能够显著降低索引维护、导航和上下文获取成本。论文强调不同操作应具有明确的有效性边界：增量视图并非在所有场景下都可替代实时分析，但在经验证一致的范围内，可提供更低延迟和更高上下文效率。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly search, navigate, and retain context from evolving repositories, but disconnected indexes, language servers, and task-local histories force repeated discovery and obscure lifecycle costs. CodeNib builds reusable lexical, dense, and structural views per repository commit, maps outputs to repository-relative source ranges, maintains selected views across edits, and serves ranked search, symbol navigation, and bounded context through one runtime.
  Across 100 snapshots, we map quality-cost frontiers across the repository-context lifecycle. When outputs match an independent rebuild, graph and vector updates are $8.7\times$ and $25.4\times$ faster at the median. On the static-navigation subset matching normalized live-server locations (63% of 1,000 requests), the median per-request live/static latency ratio is $4.7\times$. Across five models, selected context policies preserve localization with 50--87% fewer trajectory tokens than paired grep/read. Together, these results support multi-view repository-context serving with explicit, operation-specific validity boundaries.

</details>


### [158] [Repositories, Contributors, and Continuity: An Empirical Study of Foundational Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25437)
*Vincent Gierisch,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过实证软件工程方法研究量子软件生态中基础仓库的社区演化，关注当单个仓库停止活跃后，其技术概念和影响力是否会通过贡献者流动、跨项目协作及后续软件项目得以延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态由学术界、工业界和开源社区共同推动，但许多软件仓库的开发周期有限。仅考察单个仓库的活跃度无法判断其提出的概念是否持续存在，因此需要从跨仓库活动和贡献者关系的角度理解知识、社区与影响力的延续机制。

Method: 作者采用既有的实证软件工程技术，选取一组具有基础性作用的量子软件仓库进行分析；结合跨仓库开发活动数据与贡献者之间的关联关系，考察社区演化、贡献者迁移模式，以及可能反映跨项目知识转移的迹象。

Result: 研究发现，贡献者会在不同量子软件项目之间迁移，并存在跨项目知识转移的证据。项目后续发展并非单一路径：部分项目形成长期维持的社区；部分贡献者将原项目概念整合至成熟生态系统；另一些项目的活动则通过新的或后续的软件工件继续延续。

Conclusion: 量子软件中的概念影响力不应仅以原始仓库是否持续活跃来衡量。概念可以通过开发者迁移、生态系统整合和衍生项目跨越仓库边界持续传播。该研究为理解量子软件生态中的知识延续与项目影响力提供了初步实证视角。

Abstract: Driven by contributions from academia, industry, and open-source communities, the quantum software ecosystem is rapidly growing. Across this ecosystem, new concepts often emerge through software artefacts accompanying scientific publications as well as through sustained development in larger communities. However, many repositories receive development efforts only over a limited period of time, raising the question whether their concepts persist beyond individual repositories. In this paper, we apply established empirical software engineering techniques to analyse a set of foundational quantum software repositories. We combine cross-repository activity with contributor relationships to study the evolution of communities. Our analysis provides empirical evidence of contributor migration patterns and indications of cross-project knowledge transfer. We observe multiple development paths: projects may evolve into sustained communities, contributors may integrate concepts into established ecosystems, or activity may continue through new and follow-up software artefacts. Our observations provide an initial empirical perspective on how concepts and influence persist across repository boundaries in quantum software ecosystems.

</details>


### [159] [Input Relation Prompting for Metamorphic Testing on Query-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25603)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Shin-Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向查询型系统（QBS）的变形测试辅助方法，通过提示测试人员识别输入与输出之间的变形关系（MR），在缺少明确测试预言和人工测试成本高的条件下支持故障检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QBS通常缺乏可直接用于验证结果正确性的真实标签或测试预言，手工构造测试并判定输出正确性耗时较高。虽然变形测试可通过检查变换前后结果是否满足预期关系来缓解预言问题，但传统应用依赖测试人员具备充分领域知识以人工定义MR，因此需要更有效的MR识别支持。

Method: 方法以辅助测试人员发现和描述MR为核心：针对原始查询输入施加满足特定语义的转换，并定义转换前后输出应满足的关系；若实际输出违反该关系，则判定可能存在缺陷。该方法不要求预先定义完整测试用例或已知的具体正确输出，并可与组合测试、模糊测试结合，以扩展输入覆盖范围和测试场景。

Result: 论文通过一个真实网页应用的案例研究说明该方法具有可应用性，并显示其能够为QBS测试人员提供MR识别支持、促进测试执行及发现潜在异常。摘要未提供具体的缺陷数量、覆盖率、效率提升比例或与基线方法的定量比较，因此其实际收益仍需结合全文实验细节进一步评估。

Conclusion: 该研究将变形测试用于缓解QBS测试中的预言缺失问题，并从“辅助识别MR”而非完全依赖预设领域知识的角度降低测试门槛。其主要价值在于为真实QBS提供可与其他自动化测试技术协同的测试框架；后续应验证其在不同类型QBS、不同查询语义和大规模测试任务中的MR质量、误报情况与成本效益。

Abstract: Testing query-based systems (QBSs) presents significant challenges due to the absence of ground truth for validation and the extensive time and effort required for manual testing. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing an approach that assists testers in identifying metamorphic relations (MRs) for metamorphic testing (MT) instead of solely and exhaustively relying on prerequisite domain knowledge. MT is an approach rising in popularity employed to alleviate the oracle problem by applying input transformation rules (MRs) to a program. The proposed approach helps the tester by prompting MRs that describe the relationships between inputs and outputs, enabling fault detection when the expected relationship is not met. Unlike traditional testing approaches, this approach does not rely on pre-defined test cases or concrete ground truth, making it suitable for the testing of real-world QBSs. Furthermore, the proposed approach can be combined with other testing methods such as combinatorial testing and fuzz testing, expanding the possibilities for QBS testing. A conducted case study of a real-world web application demonstrates the applicability and potential of the proposed approach. Overall, this research contributes to advancing the field of metamorphic testing and provides a valuable tool for QBS testers to enhance their testing efficiency.

</details>


### [160] [SkillGate: Cost Efficient Runtime Malicious Skill File Detection in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25619)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillGate，一种面向AI编程代理“技能文件”安装环节的安全网关。其通过正则预筛选与LLM判别器结合，在SkillsBench基准上实现F1=0.817、误报率1.13%，并较全文件LLM审查减少77%的输入令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理可加载来自公共注册表的Markdown技能文件，以适配项目API、框架规范和工作流；但此类文件缺乏充分安全审查，可能通过隐蔽指令改变代理行为、泄露凭据、在生成代码中植入风险逻辑或将操作导向非预期端点。因此，技能包安装过程构成新的软件供应链风险，而现有工具链缺少系统性防护。

Method: SkillGate部署在技能包安装前，采用两阶段检测：第一阶段以正则规则识别风险信号；具有安全信号的文件直接放行，避免调用LLM；被标记的文件仅提取命中位置周围的片段窗口发送至LLM判别器，而非提交完整文件。论文在SkillsBench数据集上评估检测性能、筛查成本、运行时开销和误报行为，并与两个既有工具比较。

Result: 在1,650个样本、其中9.1%为恶意样本的SkillsBench上，SkillGate取得0.817的F1和1.13%的假阳性率。相对于完整文件LLM筛查，其LLM输入令牌减少77%。在阈值无关的AUPRC指标上，SkillGate达到0.830，显著高于两个对比工具的0.144和0.162，约为后者的5至6倍。

Conclusion: SkillGate表明，结合轻量规则过滤与局部上下文LLM判别，可在维持较强恶意技能文件检测能力的同时显著降低推理成本。该方案适合作为AI编程代理技能包安装前的网关式控制措施；不过，摘要未说明规则对新型规避技术的稳健性、LLM模型与提示配置、跨注册表泛化能力，以及漏报样本的具体风险类别，仍需在完整论文中进一步验证。

Abstract: Software engineering teams now deploy AI coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, GitHub Copilot) as first-class productivity tools, installing domain-specific skill files to tailor agent behavior to project APIs, framework conventions, and organizational workflows. These complex Markdown files are easily downloaded from public registries with a single npx skills add command and no real security screening, representing a novel supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill file can silently reprogram agent behavior, exfiltrating credentials, injecting backdoors into generated code, or redirecting agent actions to attacker-controlled endpoints. The threat is not hypothetical: recent reports document hundreds of malicious skill packages in public registries, including organized campaigns that distributed credential-stealing infostealers via fake productivity skills. No systematic toolchain defense exists for this attack surface. We present SkillGate, a deployable security gateway that screens AI skill packages before coding agent installation. SkillGate uses a hybrid regex-prefilter + LLM-judge pipeline: safe-signal files bypass the LLM entirely (skip savings); flagged files have only their matched snippet windows sent to the judge, not the full content (snippet savings). We answer four research questions covering detection effectiveness, screening cost, runtime overhead, and false positive behavior on the SkillsBench benchmark against two existing tools. On SkillsBench (n=1,650, 9.1% malicious), SkillGate achieves F1=0.817, FPR=1.13% while reducing LLM input tokens by 77% vs. full-file screening, and outperforming existing tools by 5-6x on threshold-independent AUPRC (0.830 vs. 0.144/0.162).

</details>


### [161] [An Empirical Study of Model Context Protocol Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25635)
*Muhammad Hamza Arshad Majeed,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对1,723个GitHub上的MCP应用进行大规模实证分析，发现应用侧在服务器配置和SDK通信方面已形成一定共识，但配置文件命名与人工监督机制仍高度不统一，尤其是阻塞式工具调用审批的采用率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP规范定义了大语言模型应用与外部工具的通信方式，却未规定应用如何配置、管理和监督MCP服务器。相较于传统包管理生态，开发者缺少统一的依赖集成约定；同时，既有研究主要关注MCP服务器，应用消费端生态仍缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 研究者从GitHub挖掘1,723个MCP应用，先基于具有代表性的样本构建MCPAppTax分类体系，再通过LLM辅助分析流水线将该分类体系应用于全量数据。分析维度包括服务器配置方式、SDK使用情况以及人工在环监督机制。

Result: 85.2%的应用通过文件配置MCP服务器，81.1%的应用使用官方SDK与服务器通信，表明这两个方面存在较强的实践收敛。然而，配置文件尚未形成统一命名规范。人工监督方面，90.8%的应用具有日志记录机制，77.2%支持启用或禁用控制；但仅37.2%的应用在工具执行前设置阻塞式人工审批，意味着多数应用中，语言模型可无条件调用所有已启用工具。

Conclusion: MCP应用生态已在文件化配置和官方SDK通信上形成初步事实标准，但应用侧规范仍不完整。特别是工具调用的审批与授权机制不足，反映出人工监督实践存在显著分化。未来可优先推动配置命名、权限边界和阻塞式审批等应用侧约定与标准化。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how large language model applications communicate with external tools, but leaves the application side unspecified: unlike traditional dependencies resolved through package managers, developers integrating MCP servers face no conventions for configuration, communication, or human oversight. This ecosystem is also under-researched, with existing work focused on servers rather than the applications consuming them. We conduct a large-scale study of 1,723 MCPApps mined from GitHub. We first derive MCPAppTax from a representative sample, then use an LLM-assisted pipeline to apply it across the full dataset, characterizing server integration across configuration, SDK use, and human-in-the-loop mechanisms. Our results show that the ecosystem has converged on some practices but not others: most MCPApps configure servers using files (85.2%) and use an official SDK (81.1%) to communicate with servers, yet no naming convention has emerged for configuration files. Human oversight diverges most, logging (90.8%) and enable/disable controls (77.2%) are common, but only 37.2% gate tool execution behind a blocking approval step, leaving the LLM able to invoke any enabled tool unconditionally in most MCPApps.

</details>


### [162] [KQFuzz: Knowledge-Guided Fuzzing for Quantum Libraries via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25647)
*Fuyuan Xia,Qixin Zhang,Chenhao Ying,Haojin Zhu,Shuai Wang,Yuan Luo,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出KQFuzz：一种面向量子程序库的知识引导型模糊测试框架。该方法结合代码库知识增强的大语言模型测试生成、适应度评估及两级变异，在Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq上取得最高18.44%的覆盖率提升，并发现13个经确认的软件缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算软件库的可靠性与正确性日益重要，但现有基于大语言模型的模糊测试方法在测试生成灵活性和执行效率上仍存在不足，难以有效探索复杂执行路径并生成高质量、多样化的量子测试程序。

Method: KQFuzz首先设计了适配量子程序的提示方案，将代码库中的综合知识注入大语言模型生成过程，以产生更贴近目标库接口、语义和实现特征的初始测试种子。随后，框架使用适应度引导的评估机制筛选和调度种子，并采用两级变异策略进一步扩展测试用例多样性、探索更复杂的执行路径。作者将该框架实现后，对Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个量子库开展模糊测试实验。

Result: 实验表明，KQFuzz相较其他先进方法具有更好的测试覆盖能力，覆盖率最高提升18.44%。在开发和测试过程中，系统共发现13个软件缺陷，这些缺陷均已得到开发者确认，其中12个已被修复。

Conclusion: 通过将代码库知识与大语言模型驱动的测试生成相结合，并辅以适应度评估和分层变异，KQFuzz能够更高效地生成有效量子程序测试用例、提升路径覆盖并发现真实缺陷。该工作表明，面向领域的软件知识增强是提升大语言模型模糊测试性能的有效方向。

Abstract: As quantum computing continually improves, ensuring the reliability and correctness of quantum libraries has become increasingly critical. To this end, many LLM-based fuzzing approaches towards quantum libraries have been proposed to uncover potential bugs. However, these methods still suffer from limitations such as insufficient flexibility and low efficiency, which hinder the progress of the quantum computing field. To address these challenges, we propose KQFuzz, a novel knowledge-guided fuzzer for quantum libraries. It leverages comprehensive codebase knowledge to ground LLM-based test generation, synergizing this with fitness-guided evaluation and two-level mutations to explore complex execution paths and trigger potential bugs. Firstly, KQFuzz introduces a novel prompting scheme tailored to quantum programs, which strategically incorporates knowledge of the codebase to efficiently generate high-quality quantum seed programs. Moreover, we develop evaluation and mutation strategies to handle the generated seed programs, facilitating efficient fuzzing execution while further enriching the diversity of the resulting test cases. We implement KQFuzz and conduct fuzzing on three popular quantum libraries, including Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, with coverage improved by up to 18.44%. During the development of KQFuzz, we discovered 13 bugs, all of which have been confirmed and 12 have already been fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [163] [Delta Debugging for Cyber-Physical Systems with Flaky Test Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25695)
*Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文针对具有随机性与非确定性行为的赛博物理系统仿真器，提出三种统计化、重复执行且考虑环境因素的 Delta Debugging 算法，用于定位最小失效诱发测试输入；实验表明，该方法可缩短调试时间，并通常提升失效复现概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Delta Debugging 依赖“相同输入产生稳定结果”的确定性假设，但现代 CPS 仿真中常存在随机优化、调度差异和仿真器非确定性，使同一测试无法稳定复现异常。由此，开发者难以判断删减测试片段后是否仍真正保留了失效原因，需要适用于随机环境的测试归约方法。

Method: 论文设计三种面向随机 CPS 的 Delta Debugging 算法，核心机制包括：对候选测试输入进行多次执行；使用统计失效分析而非单次结果判断是否保留失效；在归约过程中纳入环境相关因素，以避免将环境变化误判为输入删减效果。方法在两个互补案例中评估：采用随机优化的工业电梯调度系统，以及具有仿真器非确定性的自主移动机器人系统。

Result: 实验显示，所提方法能够在保持原始失效行为的前提下显著减少调试所需时间。一个关键发现是，归约后的最小失效诱发输入往往比原始输入具有更高的失效复现率：删除引入偶发随机效应的执行片段后，测试能更集中地保留导致失效的因果条件。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，Delta Debugging 在引入统计判定、重复运行和环境感知后，可有效扩展至随机性 CPS 仿真场景。其价值不仅在于生成更小、更易分析的失效测试，还在于降低执行波动对复现的影响，为 CPS 仿真调试提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is widely used to validate Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet modern CPS simulators frequently exhibit non-deterministic (flaky) behavior, making failures difficult to reproduce and debug. Although delta debugging has proven effective for deterministic systems, its underlying assumptions do not hold in stochastic environments. This paper presents three delta debugging algorithms that combine statistical failure analysis, repeated executions, and environment-aware reduction to isolate minimal failure-inducing test inputs for stochastic CPSs.
